import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Circle,
  Copy,
  ExternalLink,
  Flame,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Repeat,
  RotateCcw,
  SkipForward,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  ActiveSelection,
  CaseMastery,
  CfopStage,
  StickeringType,
  TopFaceColor,
  TwistyCoarseTimelineInfo,
  TwistyCurrentMoveInfo,
  TwistyPlayerElement,
  VisualStage,
} from '../types/cfop';
import { invertAlg, mirrorAlgLR, splitAlgMoves } from '../utils/algHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../utils/haptics';
import { AlgNotationBadges } from './AlgNotationBadges';
import { Cube2DPreview } from './Cube2DPreview';

export interface CubeViewerNavigation {
  currentIndex: number;
  total: number;
  prevLabel?: string;
  nextLabel?: string;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
}

interface CubeViewerProps {
  selection: ActiveSelection;
  isLeftyGlobal?: boolean;
  /** Estado de dominio del caso actual (Nuevo / Practicando / Dominado) */
  mastery?: CaseMastery;
  onCycleMastery?: () => void;
  /** Navegación continua Anterior / Siguiente sobre la lista filtrada activa */
  navigation?: CubeViewerNavigation;
  /** Desactiva atajos de teclado globales (ej. cuando la pestaña Timer está activa) */
  shortcutsEnabled?: boolean;
  /** Orientación del cubo: cara superior Amarilla (por defecto, z2) o Blanca */
  topFace?: TopFaceColor;
  onToggleTopFace?: () => void;
}

/**
 * Máscaras exactas de órbitas 3x3x3 de cubing.js para cada orientación:
 * - En 'yellow' (setupAlg="z2"), las piezas amarillas (índices D: 4..7, centro 5) quedan arriba (U)
 *   y las blancas (índices U: 0..3, centro 0) abajo (D).
 * - En 'white' (setupAlg=""), las piezas blancas quedan arriba (U) y las amarillas abajo (D).
 */
const STICKERING_MASKS: Record<TopFaceColor, Record<StickeringType, string>> = {
  yellow: {
    full: 'EDGES:------------,CORNERS:--------,CENTERS:------',
    OLL: 'EDGES:DDDDOOOODDDD,CORNERS:DDDDOOOO,CENTERS:DDDDD-',
    PLL: 'EDGES:DDDDPPPPDDDD,CORNERS:DDDDPPPP,CENTERS:DDDDDD',
    F2L: 'EDGES:----IIII----,CORNERS:----IIII,CENTERS:-----I',
    Cross: 'EDGES:----IIIIIIII,CORNERS:IIIIIIII,CENTERS:-DDDDD',
  },
  white: {
    full: 'EDGES:------------,CORNERS:--------,CENTERS:------',
    OLL: 'EDGES:OOOODDDDDDDD,CORNERS:OOOODDDD,CENTERS:-DDDDD',
    PLL: 'EDGES:PPPPDDDDDDDD,CORNERS:PPPPDDDD,CENTERS:DDDDDD',
    F2L: 'EDGES:IIII--------,CORNERS:IIII----,CENTERS:I-----',
    Cross: 'EDGES:IIII----IIII,CORNERS:IIIIIIII,CENTERS:DDDDD-',
  },
};

const SPEED_SEGMENTS = [
  { value: 0.25, label: '0.25x' },
  { value: 0.5, label: '0.5x' },
  { value: 1, label: '1x' },
  { value: 1.5, label: '1.5x' },
  { value: 2, label: '2x' },
] as const;

type CameraPreset = 'right' | 'left' | 'top';

const CAMERA_PRESETS: Record<
  CameraPreset,
  { short: string; lat: number; lon: number }
> = {
  right: { short: 'DER', lat: 32, lon: 32 },
  left: { short: 'IZQ', lat: 32, lon: -32 },
  top: { short: 'SUP', lat: 58, lon: 20 },
};

const STAGE_ACCENTS: Record<
  CfopStage,
  { label: string; dotClass: string; textClass: string }
> = {
  Notation: { label: 'WCA', dotClass: 'bg-[#FAFAFA]', textClass: 'text-[#FAFAFA]' },
  Reducido: { label: 'REDUCIDO', dotClass: 'bg-[#10B981]', textClass: 'text-[#10B981]' },
  Cross: { label: 'CROSS', dotClass: 'bg-[#F4F4F5]', textClass: 'text-[#F4F4F5]' },
  F2L: { label: 'F2L', dotClass: 'bg-[#3B82F6]', textClass: 'text-[#3B82F6]' },
  OLL: { label: 'OLL', dotClass: 'bg-[#EAB308]', textClass: 'text-[#EAB308]' },
  PLL: { label: 'PLL', dotClass: 'bg-[#EF4444]', textClass: 'text-[#EF4444]' },
  Timer: { label: 'TIMER', dotClass: 'bg-[#FAFAFA]', textClass: 'text-[#FAFAFA]' },
};

const CTRL_BTN =
  'inline-flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:bg-[#16161A] hover:text-[#FAFAFA] active:bg-[#1C1C21] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-35 lg:h-9 lg:w-9';

const CHIP_BTN =
  'inline-flex h-8 items-center rounded-md border px-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors lg:h-6 lg:px-2 lg:text-[10px]';

function chipClass(active: boolean): string {
  return `${CHIP_BTN} ${
    active
      ? 'border-[#FAFAFA] bg-[#FAFAFA] text-[#09090B]'
      : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]/90 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
  }`;
}

const IsolatedTwistyHost = React.memo(
  React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, { hidden?: boolean }>(({ hidden }, ref) => (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={`absolute inset-0 select-none ${
        hidden ? 'pointer-events-none invisible opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'
      }`}
    />
  ))
);
IsolatedTwistyHost.displayName = 'IsolatedTwistyHost';

export const CubeViewer: React.FC<CubeViewerProps> = ({
  selection,
  isLeftyGlobal,
  mastery,
  onCycleMastery,
  navigation,
  shortcutsEnabled = true,
  topFace,
  onToggleTopFace,
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const playerRef = useRef<TwistyPlayerElement | null>(null);
  const loopTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const swipeStartRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const [internalTopFace, setInternalTopFace] = useState<TopFaceColor>('yellow');
  const effectiveTopFace: TopFaceColor = topFace ?? internalTopFace;
  const handleToggleOrientation = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    if (onToggleTopFace) {
      onToggleTopFace();
    } else {
      setInternalTopFace((prev) => (prev === 'yellow' ? 'white' : 'yellow'));
    }
  }, [onToggleTopFace]);

  const [playerReady, setPlayerReady] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [cdnTimedOut, setCdnTimedOut] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [tempoScale, setTempoScale] = useState<number>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = window.localStorage.getItem('cfop_tempo_scale');
      const parsed = saved ? Number(saved) : 1;
      if ([0.25, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2].includes(parsed)) return parsed;
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    return 1;
  });
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'3D' | '2D'>('3D');
  const [cameraPreset, setCameraPreset] = useState<CameraPreset>('right');
  const [isMirrored, setIsMirrored] = useState<boolean>(Boolean(isLeftyGlobal));
  const [selectedAlgIndex, setSelectedAlgIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [forceFullStickering, setForceFullStickering] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showDetails, setShowDetails] = useState<boolean>(
    () =>
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1024px)').matches
  );

  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isLooping, setIsLooping] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [atStart, setAtStart] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [atEnd, setAtEnd] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [activeMoveIndex, setActiveMoveIndex] = useState<number>(-1);
  const [completedMoves, setCompletedMoves] = useState<number>(0);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showSetup, setShowSetup] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedSetup, setCopiedSetup] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const isLoopingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  isLoopingRef.current = isLooping;

  const clearLoopTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (loopTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(loopTimerRef.current);
      loopTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMirrored(Boolean(isLeftyGlobal));
    setSelectedAlgIndex(0);
    setForceFullStickering(false);
  }, [selection.id, isLeftyGlobal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    clearLoopTimer();
    setIsLooping(false);
    setShowSetup(false);
  }, [selection.id, clearLoopTimer]);

  const availableAlgs = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = [
      selection.alg,
      ...(selection.altAlgs && selection.altAlgs.length > 0
        ? selection.altAlgs
        : selection.altAlg
        ? [selection.altAlg]
        : []),
    ].filter(Boolean);
    return Array.from(new Set(raw));
  }, [selection.alg, selection.altAlg, selection.altAlgs]);

  const baseAlg = availableAlgs[selectedAlgIndex] ?? selection.alg;
  const effectiveAlg = useMemo(
    () => (isMirrored ? mirrorAlgLR(baseAlg) : baseAlg),
    [baseAlg, isMirrored]
  );

  const setupAlg = useMemo(() => invertAlg(effectiveAlg), [effectiveAlg]);

  const moves = useMemo(() => splitAlgMoves(effectiveAlg), [effectiveAlg]);

  const effectiveStickering: StickeringType = forceFullStickering
    ? 'full'
    : selection.stickering;
  const effectiveSetupAlg = effectiveTopFace === 'yellow' ? 'z2' : '';
  const effectiveStickeringMask =
    STICKERING_MASKS[effectiveTopFace][effectiveStickering];

  const latestPropsRef = useRef({
    alg: effectiveAlg,
    setupAlg: effectiveSetupAlg,
    setupAnchor: selection.setupAnchor,
    stickering: effectiveStickering,
    stickeringMask: effectiveStickeringMask,
    tempoScale,
  });
  latestPropsRef.current = {
    alg: effectiveAlg,
    setupAlg: effectiveSetupAlg,
    setupAnchor: selection.setupAnchor,
    stickering: effectiveStickering,
    stickeringMask: effectiveStickeringMask,
    tempoScale,
  };

  // Montaje único del Web Component <twisty-player>
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    const container = containerRef.current;
    let removeListeners: (() => void) | null = null;

    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (isMounted && !playerRef.current) {
        setCdnTimedOut(true);
      }
    }, 6000);

    const mountSinglePlayer = async () => {
      try {
        if (typeof customElements !== 'undefined') {
          await customElements.whenDefined('twisty-player');
        }
        if (!isMounted || !containerRef.current || playerRef.current) return;

        window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        setCdnTimedOut(false);

        const initial = latestPropsRef.current;
        const player = document.createElement(
          'twisty-player'
        ) as TwistyPlayerElement;

        player.setAttribute('puzzle', '3x3x3');
        player.visualization = '3D';
        player.background = 'none';
        player.hintFacelets = 'floating';
        player.controlPanel = 'none';
        player.experimentalSetupAlg = initial.setupAlg;
        player.experimentalSetupAnchor = initial.setupAnchor;
        player.experimentalStickering = initial.stickering;
        player.experimentalStickeringMaskOrbits = initial.stickeringMask;
        player.alg = initial.alg;
        player.tempoScale = initial.tempoScale;
        player.cameraLatitude = CAMERA_PRESETS.right.lat;
        player.cameraLongitude = CAMERA_PRESETS.right.lon;

        try {
          player.indexer = 'tree';
        } catch {
          // Propiedad opcional
        }

        player.style.display = 'grid';
        player.style.width = '100%';
        player.style.height = '100%';

        containerRef.current.appendChild(player);
        playerRef.current = player;

        const onCoarseTimeline = (info: TwistyCoarseTimelineInfo) => {
          if (!isMounted) return;
          setIsPlaying(Boolean(info.playing));
          setAtStart(Boolean(info.atStart));
          setAtEnd(Boolean(info.atEnd));
        };

        const onMoveInfo = (moveInfo: TwistyCurrentMoveInfo) => {
          if (!isMounted) return;
          const finishingLen = moveInfo.movesFinishing?.length ?? 0;
          const finishedLen = moveInfo.movesFinished?.length ?? 0;
          const doneCount =
            (moveInfo.patternIndex ?? 0) + finishingLen + finishedLen;

          setCompletedMoves(doneCount);
          if ((moveInfo.currentMoves?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
            setActiveMoveIndex(doneCount);
          } else if (doneCount > 0) {
            setActiveMoveIndex(doneCount - 1);
          } else {
            setActiveMoveIndex(-1);
          }
        };

        const coarseProp = player.experimentalModel?.coarseTimelineInfo;
        const moveProp = player.experimentalModel?.currentMoveInfo;

        coarseProp?.addFreshListener(onCoarseTimeline);
        moveProp?.addFreshListener(onMoveInfo);

        removeListeners = () => {
          try {
            coarseProp?.removeFreshListener(onCoarseTimeline);
            moveProp?.removeFreshListener(onMoveInfo);
          } catch {
            // Ignorar errores de limpieza
          }
        };

        if (isMounted) {
          setPlayerReady(true);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[CubeViewer] Error inicializando twisty-player:', err);
        if (isMounted) {
          setCdnTimedOut(true);
        }
      }
    };

    mountSinglePlayer();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      if (loopTimerRef.current !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(loopTimerRef.current);
        loopTimerRef.current = null;
      }
      removeListeners?.();
      try {
        const currentPlayer = playerRef.current;
        if (container && currentPlayer && currentPlayer.parentNode === container) {
          container.replaceChildren();
        }
      } catch {
        // Evita DOMException NotFoundError en Safari
      }
      playerRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // Sincronización reactiva de propiedades sin tocar el DOM
  useEffect(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player || !playerReady) return;

    try {
      player.pause?.();
      player.experimentalSetupAlg = effectiveSetupAlg;
      player.experimentalSetupAnchor = selection.setupAnchor;
      player.experimentalStickering = effectiveStickering;
      player.experimentalStickeringMaskOrbits = effectiveStickeringMask;
      player.alg = effectiveAlg;
      player.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
      setActiveMoveIndex(-1);
      setCompletedMoves(0);

      if (selection.autoPlayNonce) {
        const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
          try {
            playerRef.current?.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
            playerRef.current?.play?.();
          } catch {
            // Ignorar interrupción
          }
        }, 90);
        return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[CubeViewer] Error actualizando propiedades:', err);
    }
  }, [
    playerReady,
    effectiveAlg,
    effectiveSetupAlg,
    selection.setupAnchor,
    effectiveStickering,
    effectiveStickeringMask,
    selection.autoPlayNonce,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem('cfop_tempo_scale', String(tempoScale));
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player || !playerReady) return;
    try {
      player.tempoScale = tempoScale;
    } catch {
      // Ignorar error transitorio
    }
  }, [playerReady, tempoScale]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player || !playerReady) return;
    const coords = CAMERA_PRESETS[cameraPreset];
    try {
      player.cameraLatitude = coords.lat;
      player.cameraLongitude = coords.lon;
    } catch {
      // Ignorar error transitorio
    }
  }, [playerReady, cameraPreset]);

  // Bucle automático manos libres (repite tras pausa breve de 850ms)
  useEffect(() => {
    clearLoopTimer();
    if (!isLooping || !playerReady || !atEnd || isPlaying) return;

    loopTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const player = playerRef.current;
      if (!player || !isLoopingRef.current) return;
      try {
        player.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
        player.play?.();
      } catch {
        // Ignorar interrupción
      }
    }, 850);

    return clearLoopTimer;
  }, [isLooping, atEnd, isPlaying, playerReady, effectiveAlg, clearLoopTimer]);

  const handleTogglePlay = useCallback(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player) return;

    triggerHaptic('medium');
    clearLoopTimer();
    try {
      if (isPlaying) {
        setIsLooping(false);
        player.pause?.();
      } else {
        if (atEnd) {
          player.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
        }
        player.play?.();
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignorar error de reproducción
    }
  }, [isPlaying, atEnd, clearLoopTimer]);

  const handleToggleLoop = useCallback(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player || !playerReady) return;
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    clearLoopTimer();

    setIsLooping((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      if (next && !isPlaying) {
        try {
          if (atEnd) {
            player.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
          }
          player.play?.();
        } catch {
          // Ignorar error de reproducción
        }
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, [atEnd, clearLoopTimer, isPlaying, playerReady]);

  const handleReset = useCallback(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player) return;
    triggerHaptic('medium');
    clearLoopTimer();
    setIsLooping(false);
    try {
      player.pause?.();
      player.jumpToStart?.({ flash: false });
      setActiveMoveIndex(-1);
      setCompletedMoves(0);
    } catch {
      // Ignorar error
    }
  }, [clearLoopTimer]);

  const handleJumpToEnd = useCallback(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;
    if (!player) return;
    triggerHaptic('medium');
    clearLoopTimer();
    setIsLooping(false);
    try {
      player.pause?.();
      player.jumpToEnd?.({ flash: false });
    } catch {
      // Ignorar error
    }
  }, [clearLoopTimer]);

  const stepPlayer = useCallback(
    (direction: 1 | -1) => {
      const player = playerRef.current;
      if (!player) return;
      triggerHaptic('light');
      clearLoopTimer();
      setIsLooping(false);
      try {
        player.controller?.animationController?.play?.({
          direction,
          untilBoundary: 'move',
          autoSkipToOtherEndIfStartingAtBoundary: false,
        });
      } catch {
        // Ignorar error
      }
    },
    [clearLoopTimer]
  );

  const handleStepForward = useCallback(() => {
    if (!atEnd) stepPlayer(1);
  }, [atEnd, stepPlayer]);

  const handleStepBackward = useCallback(() => {
    if (!atStart) stepPlayer(-1);
  }, [atStart, stepPlayer]);

  // Atajos de teclado: Space (Play/Pausa), ← / → (Paso a paso), R (Reiniciar)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!shortcutsEnabled) return;

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (!playerReady) return;
      if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;

      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (
        target &&
        (target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
          target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
          target.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
          target.isContentEditable)
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleTogglePlay();
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleStepForward();
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleStepBackward();
      } else if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleReset();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [
    shortcutsEnabled,
    playerReady,
    handleTogglePlay,
    handleStepForward,
    handleStepBackward,
    handleReset,
  ]);

  const handleJumpToMove = useCallback(
    async (index: number) => {
      const player = playerRef.current;
      if (!player) return;
      triggerHaptic('light');
      clearLoopTimer();
      setIsLooping(false);
      try {
        player.pause?.();
        const indexer = await player.experimentalModel?.indexer?.get();
        if (indexer) {
          player.timestamp =
            indexer.indexToMoveStartTimestamp(index) +
            indexer.moveDuration(index);
        }
      } catch {
        // Ignorar error transitorio
      }
    },
    [clearLoopTimer]
  );

  const handleToggleMirror = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    setIsMirrored((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      setCameraPreset(next ? 'left' : 'right');
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const handleCycleAltAlg = useCallback(() => {
    if (availableAlgs.length <= 1) return;
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    setSelectedAlgIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % availableAlgs.length);
  }, [availableAlgs.length]);

  const handleCopyAlg = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(effectiveAlg);
      triggerHaptic('success');
      setCopied(true);
      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);
    } catch {
      // Portapapeles no disponible
    }
  }, [effectiveAlg]);

  const handleCopySetup = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(setupAlg);
      triggerHaptic('success');
      setCopiedSetup(true);
      window.setTimeout(() => setCopiedSetup(false), 1600);
    } catch {
      // Portapapeles no disponible
    }
  }, [setupAlg]);

  // Swipe horizontal seguro solo en cabecera y tira de secuencia (nunca en el visor 3D)
  const handleSwipeStart = useCallback((e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (e.touches.length !== 1) return;
    swipeStartRef.current = {
      x: e.touches[0].clientX,
      y: e.touches[0].clientY,
    };
  }, []);

  const handleSwipeEnd = useCallback(
    (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
      const start = swipeStartRef.current;
      swipeStartRef.current = null;
      if (!start || !navigation || navigation.total <= 1) return;
      const touch = e.changedTouches[0];
      if (!touch) return;
      const dx = touch.clientX - start.x;
      const dy = touch.clientY - start.y;
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 56 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) * 1.5) {
        if (dx < 0) {
          navigation.onNext();
        } else {
          navigation.onPrev();
        }
      }
    },
    [navigation]
  );

  const stageAccent = STAGE_ACCENTS[selection.stage] ?? STAGE_ACCENTS.Notation;
  const previewStageFor2D: VisualStage =
    selection.visualStage ??
    (selection.stage === 'Notation' ||
    selection.stage === 'Reducido' ||
    selection.stage === 'Timer'
      ? 'F2L'
      : selection.stage);
  const isNotation = selection.stage === 'Notation';

  const effectiveMastery: CaseMastery = mastery ?? 'unlearned';

  const masteryMeta =
    effectiveMastery === 'mastered'
      ? {
          label: 'Dominado',
          className:
            'border-[#10B981]/50 bg-[#10B981]/15 text-[#10B981] hover:bg-[#10B981]/20',
          title: 'Dominado · Toca para reiniciar estado',
        }
      : effectiveMastery === 'practicing'
      ? {
          label: 'Practicando',
          className:
            'border-amber-500/50 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500/20',
          title: 'Practicando · Toca para marcar como Dominado',
        }
      : {
          label: 'Nuevo',
          className:
            'border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-zinc-200',
          title: 'Por aprender · Toca para marcar como Practicando',
        };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]">
      {/* Cabecera del caso (soporta swipe horizontal seguro para Anterior/Siguiente) */}
      <div
        onTouchStart={handleSwipeStart}
        onTouchEnd={handleSwipeEnd}
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-[#222226] px-3 py-2"
      >
        <span
          translate="no"
          className={`shrink-0 font-mono text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums ${stageAccent.textClass}`}
        >
          {selection.code}
        </span>
        <h2 className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">
          {selection.title}
        </h2>
        {selection.sectionBadge && (
          <span className="hidden rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-300 sm:inline">
            {selection.sectionBadge}
          </span>
        )}
        {!selection.sectionBadge && selection.twoLook && (
          <span className="hidden rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-300 sm:inline">
            2-LOOK
          </span>
        )}
        {isMirrored && (
          <span className="rounded border border-[#3B82F6]/40 bg-[#3B82F6]/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium text-[#3B82F6]">
            L↔R
          </span>
        )}
        {onCycleMastery && !isNotation && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCycleMastery}
            title={masteryMeta.title}
            aria-label={`Estado de aprendizaje: ${masteryMeta.label}. Toca para cambiar`}
            className={`inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${masteryMeta.className}`}
          >
            {effectiveMastery === 'mastered' ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            ) : effectiveMastery === 'practicing' ? (
              <Flame className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            ) : (
              <Circle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>{masteryMeta.label}</span>
          </button>
        )}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleCopyAlg}
          aria-label="Copiar algoritmo"
          title="Copiar algoritmo"
          className="inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-400 transition-colors hover:bg-[#16161A] hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
        >
          {copied ? (
            <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#10B981]" />
          ) : (
            <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          )}
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">{copied ? 'Copiado' : 'Copiar'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Viewport 3D / 2D con etiquetas y chip compacto de orientación U en la esquina */}
      <div className="relative h-[clamp(180px,28dvh,240px)] border-b border-[#222226] bg-[#0C0C0E] lg:h-64">
        <IsolatedTwistyHost ref={containerRef} hidden={viewMode !== '3D'} />

        {viewMode === '2D' && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Cube2DPreview
              alg={effectiveAlg}
              stage={previewStageFor2D}
              topFace={effectiveTopFace}
              className="h-40 w-40 p-1.5"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {viewMode === '3D' && !playerReady && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-500">
              {cdnTimedOut ? 'Sin conexión con cdn.cubing.net' : 'Cargando 3D…'}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Superior izquierda: etapa + HTM */}
        <div
          translate="no"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute top-2.5 left-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-400"
        >
          <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${stageAccent.dotClass}`} />
          <span className="text-zinc-300">{stageAccent.label}</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-700">/</span>
          <span>{moves.length} HTM</span>
        </div>

        {/* Superior derecha: Orientación U (Amarilla/Blanca) + 3D / 2D */}
        <div
          translate="no"
          className="absolute top-2 right-2 z-10 flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleOrientation}
            aria-label={
              effectiveTopFace === 'yellow'
                ? 'Cara superior Amarilla. Toca para cambiar a Blanca arriba'
                : 'Cara superior Blanca. Toca para cambiar a Amarilla arriba'
            }
            title={
              effectiveTopFace === 'yellow'
                ? 'Cara arriba (U): Amarilla · Toca para cambiar a Blanca'
                : 'Cara arriba (U): Blanca · Toca para cambiar a Amarilla'
            }
            className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]/90 px-2 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA] lg:h-7"
          >
            <span className="text-zinc-500">U:</span>
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className={`h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-[2px] border ${
                effectiveTopFace === 'yellow'
                  ? 'border-zinc-900/40 bg-[#EAB308]'
                  : 'border-zinc-500 bg-[#FAFAFA]'
              }`}
            />
          </button>

          <div className="inline-flex rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]/90 p-0.5">
            {(['3D', '2D'] as const).map((mode) => (
              <button
                key={mode}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setViewMode(mode);
                }}
                className={`h-7 rounded px-2 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors lg:h-6 ${
                  viewMode === mode
                    ? 'bg-[#FAFAFA] text-[#09090B]'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                {mode}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Inferior izquierda: cámara */}
        {viewMode === '3D' && (
          <div
            translate="no"
            className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 z-10 inline-flex rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]/90 p-0.5"
          >
            {(['right', 'left', 'top'] as const).map((preset) => (
              <button
                key={preset}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setCameraPreset(preset);
                }}
                aria-label={`Cámara ${CAMERA_PRESETS[preset].short}`}
                className={`h-7 rounded px-2 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors lg:h-6 ${
                  cameraPreset === preset
                    ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-[#FAFAFA]'
                    : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-300'
                }`}
              >
                {CAMERA_PRESETS[preset].short}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Inferior derecha: espejo / alternativo / máscara */}
        <div translate="no" className="absolute right-2 bottom-2 z-10 flex gap-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleMirror}
            aria-pressed={isMirrored}
            title="Espejo izquierda ↔ derecha"
            className={chipClass(isMirrored)}
          >
            L↔R
          </button>
          {availableAlgs.length > 1 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCycleAltAlg}
              aria-pressed={selectedAlgIndex > 0}
              title="Cambiar variante de algoritmo"
              className={chipClass(selectedAlgIndex > 0)}
            >
              {selectedAlgIndex === 0
                ? `ALT (${availableAlgs.length - 1})`
                : `ALT ${selectedAlgIndex}/${availableAlgs.length - 1}`}
            </button>
          )}
          {selection.stickering !== 'full' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                triggerHaptic('selection');
                setForceFullStickering((prev) => !prev);
              }}
              aria-pressed={forceFullStickering}
              title="Mostrar todos los colores"
              className={chipClass(forceFullStickering)}
            >
              FULL
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Selector explícito de algoritmos alternativos cuando el caso tiene variantes */}
      {availableAlgs.length > 1 && (
        <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto border-b border-[#222226] bg-[#0C0C0E] px-3 py-1.5">
          <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
            VARIANTE:
          </span>
          {availableAlgs.map((algVariant, idx) => {
            const active = selectedAlgIndex === idx;
            const label =
              selection.altAlgLabels?.[idx] ??
              (idx === 0 ? 'Principal' : `Alt ${idx}`);
            return (
              <button
                key={idx}
                type="button"
                title={isMirrored ? mirrorAlgLR(algVariant) : algVariant}
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setSelectedAlgIndex(idx);
                }}
                className={`inline-flex h-6 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded border px-2 font-mono text-[11px] transition-colors ${
                  active
                    ? 'border-[#FAFAFA] bg-[#FAFAFA] font-semibold text-[#09090B]'
                    : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#141418] text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Guía Ergonómica Técnica: Agarre Inicial, AUF y Video Timestamp (CFOP Reducido) */}
      {(selection.grip || selection.auf || selection.videoUrl) && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1 border-b border-[#222226] bg-[#0C0C0E] px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-400">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5">
            {selection.grip && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-600">AGARRE:</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-200">{selection.grip}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {selection.auf && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-600">AUF:</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-200">{selection.auf}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          {selection.videoUrl && selection.videoTimestamp && (
            <a
              href={selection.videoUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded border border-[#10B981]/40 bg-[#10B981]/10 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium text-[#10B981] transition-colors hover:bg-[#10B981]/20"
            >
              <span>▶ {selection.videoTimestamp} · Video JS cuber</span>
              <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
            </a>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Secuencia Karaoke con Chunking Cognitivo + Setup Inverso + Swipe horizontal */}
      <div
        onTouchStart={handleSwipeStart}
        onTouchEnd={handleSwipeEnd}
        className="border-b border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] px-3 py-2"
      >
        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500">
          <span>Secuencia (toca un paso)</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {(!isNotation || moves.length > 1) && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setShowSetup((prev) => !prev);
                }}
                aria-expanded={showSetup}
                title="Mostrar algoritmo inverso para armar este caso desde un cubo resuelto"
                className={`inline-flex h-6 items-center gap-1 rounded border px-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  showSetup
                    ? 'border-[#FAFAFA] bg-[#FAFAFA] text-[#09090B]'
                    : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]'
                }`}
              >
                <span>Setup</span>
              </button>
            )}
            <span translate="no" className="text-zinc-400">
              {Math.min(completedMoves, moves.length)}/{moves.length} HTM
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {showSetup && (
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-2.5 py-1.5">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <span className="block font-mono text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
                Setup desde cubo resuelto (inverso)
              </span>
              <p
                translate="no"
                className="truncate font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-200"
              >
                {setupAlg}
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopySetup}
              aria-label="Copiar setup inverso"
              title="Copiar setup inverso"
              className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
            >
              {copiedSetup ? (
                <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#10B981]" />
              ) : (
                <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <AlgNotationBadges
          alg={effectiveAlg}
          activeMoveIndex={activeMoveIndex}
          completedMoves={completedMoves}
          onMoveClick={handleJumpToMove}
          autoScroll
        />
      </div>

      {/* Controles de reproducción manos libres (Play + Bucle + Paso a paso) + Velocidad + Anterior/Siguiente */}
      <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-[#222226] px-3 py-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleReset}
            disabled={!playerReady}
            aria-label="Reiniciar"
            title="Reiniciar (R)"
            className={CTRL_BTN}
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepBackward}
            disabled={!playerReady || atStart}
            aria-label="Paso anterior"
            title="Paso anterior (←)"
            className={CTRL_BTN}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleTogglePlay}
            disabled={!playerReady}
            title="Reproducir / Pausar (Space)"
            className="inline-flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#FAFAFA] px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#09090B] transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-200 active:bg-zinc-300 disabled:opacity-40 lg:h-9 lg:text-xs"
          >
            {isPlaying ? (
              <>
                <Pause className="h-4 w-4 fill-current" />
                <span>Pausa</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Play className="h-4 w-4 fill-current" />
                <span>{atEnd ? 'Repetir' : 'Play'}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleLoop}
            disabled={!playerReady}
            aria-pressed={isLooping}
            aria-label={isLooping ? 'Desactivar bucle automático' : 'Activar bucle automático'}
            title={
              isLooping
                ? 'Bucle activo · Se repite automáticamente manos libres'
                : 'Activar repetición en bucle manos libres'
            }
            className={`inline-flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border transition-colors disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-35 lg:h-9 lg:w-9 ${
              isLooping
                ? 'border-[#10B981] bg-[#10B981]/20 text-[#10B981]'
                : 'border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-300 hover:bg-[#16161A] hover:text-[#FAFAFA] active:bg-[#1C1C21]'
            }`}
          >
            <Repeat className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepForward}
            disabled={!playerReady || atEnd}
            aria-label="Paso siguiente"
            title="Paso siguiente (→)"
            className={CTRL_BTN}
          >
            <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleJumpToEnd}
            disabled={!playerReady}
            aria-label="Ir al final"
            title="Ir al final"
            className={`${CTRL_BTN} hidden sm:inline-flex`}
          >
            <SkipForward className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div
          translate="no"
          role="radiogroup"
          aria-label="Velocidad"
          className="grid grid-cols-5 rounded-md border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] p-0.5"
        >
          {SPEED_SEGMENTS.map((seg) => {
            const active = Math.abs(tempoScale - seg.value) < 0.03;
            return (
              <button
                key={seg.value}
                type="button"
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={active}
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setTempoScale(seg.value);
                }}
                className={`h-8 rounded font-mono text-xs font-medium tabular-nums transition-colors lg:h-7 ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-[#FAFAFA] font-semibold text-[#09090B]'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                {seg.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Navegación continua Anterior / Siguiente sobre la lista filtrada */}
        {navigation && navigation.total > 1 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={navigation.onPrev}
              aria-label="Caso anterior"
              className="inline-flex h-10 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-start gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] px-2.5 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA] active:bg-[#1C1C21] lg:h-8"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-zinc-400" />
              <span className="truncate">
                {navigation.prevLabel ?? 'Anterior'}
              </span>
            </button>

            <span
              translate="no"
              className="shrink-0 px-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500"
            >
              {navigation.currentIndex + 1}/{navigation.total}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={navigation.onNext}
              aria-label="Caso siguiente"
              className="inline-flex h-10 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-end gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] px-2.5 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA] active:bg-[#1C1C21] lg:h-8"
            >
              <span className="truncate">
                {navigation.nextLabel ?? 'Siguiente'}
              </span>
              <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-zinc-400" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Atajos de teclado: solo en pantallas con teclado */}
        <div className="hidden items-center justify-center gap-4 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 lg:flex">
          {[
            { keys: ['Espacio'], label: 'Play/Pausa' },
            { keys: ['←', '→'], label: 'Paso a paso' },
            { keys: ['R'], label: 'Reiniciar' },
          ].map((hint) => (
            <span key={hint.label} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
              {hint.keys.map((k) => (
                <kbd
                  key={k}
                  className="rounded border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {k}
                </kbd>
              ))}
              <span>{hint.label}</span>
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detalles colapsables (abiertos por defecto en desktop) */}
      <div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            triggerHaptic('selection');
            setShowDetails((prev) => !prev);
          }}
          aria-expanded={showDetails}
          className="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-between px-3 text-left lg:h-10"
        >
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-300">
            {isNotation ? 'Cómo se hace' : 'Reconocimiento y ejecución'}
          </span>
          <ChevronDown
            className={`h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 transition-transform ${
              showDetails ? 'rotate-180' : ''
            }`}
          />
        </button>

        {showDetails && (
          <dl className="space-y-2.5 border-t border-[#222226] px-3 py-3 text-[13px] leading-relaxed lg:text-xs">
            <div>
              <dt className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
                {isNotation ? 'Movimiento' : 'Reconocer'}
              </dt>
              <dd className="mt-0.5 text-zinc-300">{selection.recognition}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
                {isNotation ? 'Dedos' : 'Ejecutar'}
              </dt>
              <dd className="mt-0.5 text-zinc-300">{selection.explanation}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
  errorMessage: string;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<
  ErrorBoundaryProps,
  ErrorBoundaryState
> {
  constructor(props: ErrorBoundaryProps) {
    super(props);
    this.state = {
      hasError: false,
      errorMessage: '',
    };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown): ErrorBoundaryState {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Error inesperado en tiempo de ejecución';
    return {
      hasError: true,
      errorMessage: message,
    };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: unknown, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo): void {
    console.error('[CFOP Master ErrorBoundary]', error, errorInfo);
  }

  handleReset = (): void => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, errorMessage: '' });
    window.location.reload();
  };

  render(): React.ReactNode {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-[#09090B] p-6 text-zinc-100">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#222226] pb-3">
              <h1 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">
                Excepción de ejecución
              </h1>
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-[#EF4444]">ERR_RUNTIME</span>
            </div>

            <pre className="mt-3 overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] p-3 font-mono text-xs text-[#EF4444]">
              {this.state.errorMessage}
            </pre>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={this.handleReset}
              className="mt-4 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#FAFAFA] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[#09090B] transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-200"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>Reiniciar interfaz</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

FILE: src/components/NotationModule.tsx
