import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  FUNDAMENTAL_TRIGGERS,
  NOTATION_CATEGORY_LABELS,
  NOTATION_MOVES,
  NOTATION_RULES,
  type TriggerDemo,
} from '../data/cfopData';
import type { NotationCategory, NotationMove } from '../types/cfop';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../utils/haptics';

interface NotationModuleProps {
  selectedId: string;
  onSelectMove: (move: NotationMove) => void;
  onSelectTrigger: (trigger: TriggerDemo) => void;
}

const CATEGORY_ORDER: NotationCategory[] = [
  'basic',
  'double',
  'slice',
  'wide',
  'rotation',
];

/** Etiqueta compacta e independiente del idioma: sentido + grados */
function compactDirection(move: string): string {
  if (move.includes('2')) return '180°';
  return move.includes("'") ? '↺ 90°' : '↻ 90°';
}

const WCA_LEGEND = [
  { face: 'U', color: 'bg-[#FAFAFA]', label: 'Arriba' },
  { face: 'F', color: 'bg-[#10B981]', label: 'Frente' },
  { face: 'R', color: 'bg-[#EF4444]', label: 'Derecha' },
  { face: 'D', color: 'bg-[#EAB308]', label: 'Abajo' },
];

export const NotationModule: React.FC<NotationModuleProps> = ({
  selectedId,
  onSelectMove,
  onSelectTrigger,
}) => {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<'all' | NotationCategory>('all');

  const visibleCategories =
    activeCategory === 'all' ? CATEGORY_ORDER : [activeCategory];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
      {/* Encabezado + Reglas de sintaxis */}
      <section className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-3 sm:p-4">
        <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-[#FAFAFA] sm:text-lg">
            Notación
          </h1>
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500">
            {NOTATION_MOVES.length} movs
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
          {NOTATION_RULES.map((rule) => (
            <div
              key={rule.symbol}
              className="rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] p-2.5"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5">
                <span
                  translate="no"
                  className="font-mono text-lg font-bold leading-none tabular-nums text-[#FAFAFA]"
                >
                  {rule.symbol}
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500">
                  {rule.badge}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-snug text-zinc-400 sm:text-xs">
                {rule.explanation}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Filtro de categorías: scroll horizontal en móvil */}
      <div className="-mx-4 px-4 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0">
        <div className="no-scrollbar flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-1">
          {(['all', ...CATEGORY_ORDER] as const).map((cat) => {
            const active = activeCategory === cat;
            const label = cat === 'all' ? 'Todos' : NOTATION_CATEGORY_LABELS[cat].label;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('selection');
                  setActiveCategory(cat);
                }}
                className={`h-8 shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-3 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-[#FAFAFA] text-[#09090B]'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:bg-[#16161A] hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Matrices de movimientos */}
      {visibleCategories.map((cat) => {
        const meta = NOTATION_CATEGORY_LABELS[cat];
        const moves = NOTATION_MOVES.filter((m) => m.category === cat);

        return (
          <section
            key={cat}
            className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-3 sm:p-4"
          >
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">{meta.label}</h2>
              <span className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
                {meta.subtitle}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 sm:grid-cols-6 sm:gap-2">
              {moves.map((item) => {
                const isActive = selectedId === item.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('light');
                      onSelectMove(item);
                    }}
                    aria-label={`${item.move}: ${item.name}`}
                    className={`flex min-h-[56px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-md border transition-colors active:bg-[#1C1C21] ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'border-[#FAFAFA] bg-[#16161A]'
                        : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] hover:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-[#16161A]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span
                      translate="no"
                      className="font-mono text-lg font-bold leading-none tabular-nums text-[#FAFAFA]"
                    >
                      {item.move}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      translate="no"
                      className="font-mono text-[10px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500"
                    >
                      {compactDirection(item.move)}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </section>
        );
      })}

      {/* Triggers fundamentales */}
      <section className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-3 sm:p-4">
        <div className="mb-2.5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">Triggers</h2>
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            Secuencias base
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5 sm:grid-cols-2 sm:gap-2">
          {FUNDAMENTAL_TRIGGERS.map((trig) => {
            const isActive = selectedId === trig.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={trig.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('light');
                  onSelectTrigger(trig);
                }}
                className={`flex min-h-[48px] items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-left transition-colors active:bg-[#1C1C21] ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'border-[#FAFAFA] bg-[#16161A]'
                    : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] hover:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-[#16161A]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#FAFAFA]">
                  {trig.name}
                </span>
                <span
                  translate="no"
                  className="font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {trig.alg}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Leyenda de orientación WCA */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-3 py-2.5">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
          Orientación
        </span>
        {WCA_LEGEND.map((item) => (
          <span key={item.face} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <span className={`h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-sm ${item.color}`} />
            <span translate="no" className="font-mono font-semibold text-zinc-200">
              {item.face}
            </span>
            <span>{item.label}</span>
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

========================================
FILE: src/components/PracticeTrainerModal.tsx
========================================
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Copy,
  Eye,
  Flame,
  Lightbulb,
  Play,
  RotateCcw,
  Shuffle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  CaseMastery,
  CfopCase,
  TopFaceColor,
  VisualStage,
} from '../types/cfop';
import { invertAlg, mirrorAlgLR } from '../utils/algHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../utils/haptics';
import { AlgNotationBadges } from './AlgNotationBadges';
import { Cube2DPreview } from './Cube2DPreview';

interface PracticeTrainerModalProps {
  cases: CfopCase[];
  stageLabel: string;
  isLeftyGlobal: boolean;
  topFace: TopFaceColor;
  getMastery: (id: string) => CaseMastery;
  onSetMastery: (id: string, status: CaseMastery) => void;
  onOpenIn3D: (cfopCase: CfopCase) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function shuffleArray<T>(items: T[]): T[] {
  const arr = [...items];
  for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
  }
  return arr;
}

function buildSmartQueue(
  cases: CfopCase[],
  getMastery: (id: string) => CaseMastery
): CfopCase[] {
  const practicing: CfopCase[] = [];
  const unlearned: CfopCase[] = [];
  const mastered: CfopCase[] = [];

  for (const c of cases) {
    const m = getMastery(c.id);
    if (m === 'practicing') practicing.push(c);
    else if (m === 'unlearned') unlearned.push(c);
    else mastered.push(c);
  }

  return [
    ...shuffleArray(practicing),
    ...shuffleArray(unlearned),
    ...shuffleArray(mastered),
  ];
}

function resolvePreviewStage(item: CfopCase): VisualStage {
  if (item.visualStage) return item.visualStage;
  if (item.stage === 'Reducido') return 'OLL';
  return item.stage;
}

export const PracticeTrainerModal: React.FC<PracticeTrainerModalProps> = ({
  cases,
  stageLabel,
  isLeftyGlobal,
  topFace,
  getMastery,
  onSetMastery,
  onOpenIn3D,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const [queue, setQueue] = useState<CfopCase[]>(() =>
    buildSmartQueue(cases, getMastery)
  );
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [isRevealed, setIsRevealed] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showSetup, setShowSetup] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showHint, setShowHint] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState<'alg' | 'setup' | null>(null);
  const [knownCount, setKnownCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [reviewCount, setReviewCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [missedIds, setMissedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set());

  // Bloquea el scroll del fondo y permite cerrar con Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  const currentCase = queue[currentIndex] ?? null;
  const isComplete = currentIndex >= queue.length;
  const totalRated = knownCount + reviewCount;
  const accuracy =
    totalRated > 0 ? Math.round((knownCount / totalRated) * 100) : null;

  const displayAlg = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentCase) return '';
    return isLeftyGlobal ? mirrorAlgLR(currentCase.alg) : currentCase.alg;
  }, [currentCase, isLeftyGlobal]);

  const setupAlg = useMemo(() => invertAlg(displayAlg), [displayAlg]);

  const resetCardUi = useCallback(() => {
    setIsRevealed(false);
    setShowSetup(false);
    setShowHint(false);
    setCopiedKey(null);
  }, []);

  const handleShuffleDeck = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    setQueue(shuffleArray(cases));
    setCurrentIndex(0);
    setKnownCount(0);
    setReviewCount(0);
    setMissedIds(new Set());
    resetCardUi();
  }, [cases, resetCardUi]);

  const handleRestartMissed = useCallback(() => {
    const missedCases = cases.filter((c) => missedIds.has(c.id));
    if (missedCases.length === 0) {
      handleShuffleDeck();
      return;
    }
    triggerHaptic('selection');
    setQueue(shuffleArray(missedCases));
    setCurrentIndex(0);
    setKnownCount(0);
    setReviewCount(0);
    setMissedIds(new Set());
    resetCardUi();
  }, [cases, handleShuffleDeck, missedIds, resetCardUi]);

  const handleCopy = useCallback(
    async (text: string, kind: 'alg' | 'setup') => {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
        triggerHaptic('success');
        setCopiedKey(kind);
        window.setTimeout(() => setCopiedKey(null), 1400);
      } catch {
        // Portapapeles no disponible
      }
    },
    []
  );

  const handleGradeReview = useCallback(() => {
    if (!currentCase) return;
    triggerHaptic('medium');
    onSetMastery(currentCase.id, 'practicing');
    setReviewCount((c) => c + 1);
    setMissedIds((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.add(currentCase.id);
      return next;
    });

    // Re-inserta el caso ~3 tarjetas después dentro de la sesión actual (spaced repetition)
    setQueue((prev) => {
      const insertAt = Math.min(prev.length, currentIndex + 4);
      const next = [...prev];
      // Evita duplicar si ya existe más adelante en la cola
      const alreadyAhead = prev
        .slice(currentIndex + 1)
        .some((item) => item.id === currentCase.id);
      if (!alreadyAhead && prev.length < cases.length * 3) {
        next.splice(insertAt, 0, currentCase);
      }
      return next;
    });

    setCurrentIndex((i) => i + 1);
    resetCardUi();
  }, [cases.length, currentCase, currentIndex, onSetMastery, resetCardUi]);

  const handleGradeKnown = useCallback(() => {
    if (!currentCase) return;
    triggerHaptic('success');
    onSetMastery(currentCase.id, 'mastered');
    setKnownCount((c) => c + 1);
    setCurrentIndex((i) => i + 1);
    resetCardUi();
  }, [currentCase, onSetMastery, resetCardUi]);

  const progressPct =
    queue.length > 0
      ? Math.min(100, Math.round((currentIndex / queue.length) * 100))
      : 100;

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={`Entrenamiento Active Recall: ${stageLabel}`}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-[#09090B]/95 backdrop-blur-md sm:items-center sm:justify-center sm:p-4"
    >
      <div className="pt-safe pb-safe-2 flex h-full w-full flex-col bg-[#09090B] sm:h-auto sm:max-h-[90dvh] sm:max-w-lg sm:overflow-hidden sm:rounded-2xl sm:border sm:border-[#222226] sm:bg-[#111113] sm:pt-0 sm:pb-0">
        {/* Cabecera de sesión */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#222226] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#10B981]">
                Active Recall
              </span>
              <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
              <span className="truncate text-xs font-medium text-zinc-300">
                {stageLabel}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-400">
              <span>
                {Math.min(currentIndex + 1, queue.length)}/{queue.length}
              </span>
              {accuracy !== null && (
                <>
                  <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
                  <span className="text-[#10B981]">{accuracy}% aciertos</span>
                </>
              )}
              {isLeftyGlobal && (
                <span className="rounded border border-[#3B82F6]/40 bg-[#3B82F6]/10 px-1 text-[10px] text-[#3B82F6]">
                  L↔R
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleShuffleDeck}
              title="Mezclar tarjetas"
              aria-label="Mezclar tarjetas"
              className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] px-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
            >
              <Shuffle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Mezclar</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                triggerHaptic('light');
                onClose();
              }}
              aria-label="Cerrar entrenador"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Barra de progreso */}
        <div className="h-1 w-full shrink-0 bg-zinc-900">
          <div
            className="h-full bg-[#10B981] transition-all duration-300"
            style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Cuerpo principal */}
        {isComplete || !currentCase ? (
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-8 text-center">
            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-[#10B981]/40 bg-[#10B981]/10 text-[#10B981]">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-7 w-7" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">
              Sesión completada
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-400">
              Practicaste {totalRated} {totalRated === 1 ? 'tarjeta' : 'tarjetas'} de{' '}
              {stageLabel}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-5 grid w-full max-w-xs grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-2.5 sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                <div className="font-mono text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums text-[#10B981]">
                  {knownCount}
                </div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase text-zinc-500">
                  Dominados
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-2.5 sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                <div className="font-mono text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-400">
                  {reviewCount}
                </div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase text-zinc-500">
                  A repasar
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-2.5 sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                <div className="font-mono text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums text-[#FAFAFA]">
                  {accuracy ?? 100}%
                </div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase text-zinc-500">
                  Precisión
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 flex w-full max-w-xs flex-col gap-2">
              {missedIds.size > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleRestartMissed}
                  className="inline-flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/15 px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-500/20"
                >
                  <Flame className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Repasar los que costaron ({missedIds.size})</span>
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleShuffleDeck}
                className="inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#FAFAFA] px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-[#09090B] transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-200"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>Otra ronda completa</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('light');
                  onClose();
                }}
                className="inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] px-4 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
              >
                Volver al catálogo
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="no-scrollbar flex flex-1 flex-col items-center overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4">
              {/* Badges sin spoiler */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="rounded border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                  {currentCase.stage === 'Reducido'
                    ? currentCase.sectionBadge ?? 'Reducido'
                    : currentCase.stage}
                </span>
                {currentCase.twoLook && (
                  <span className="rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-zinc-300">
                    2-LOOK
                  </span>
                )}
                {getMastery(currentCase.id) === 'practicing' && (
                  <span className="rounded border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-400">
                    Practicando
                  </span>
                )}
                {getMastery(currentCase.id) === 'mastered' && (
                  <span className="rounded border border-[#10B981]/40 bg-[#10B981]/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-[#10B981]">
                    Dominado
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Diagrama 2D grande para reconocimiento instantáneo */}
              <div className="my-4">
                <Cube2DPreview
                  alg={displayAlg}
                  stage={resolvePreviewStage(currentCase)}
                  topFace={topFace}
                  className="h-44 w-44 p-2 sm:h-48 sm:w-48"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Botones compactos de apoyo sin spoiler: Setup (Inverso) y Pista */}
              <div className="flex w-full max-w-md items-center justify-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('selection');
                    setShowSetup((prev) => !prev);
                  }}
                  aria-expanded={showSetup}
                  className={`inline-flex h-9 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-3 font-mono text-xs transition-colors ${
                    showSetup
                      ? 'border-zinc-500 bg-zinc-800 text-[#FAFAFA]'
                      : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-700 sm:bg-[#09090B]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>Setup (Inverso)</span>
                </button>

                {!isRevealed && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('selection');
                      setShowHint((prev) => !prev);
                    }}
                    aria-expanded={showHint}
                    className={`inline-flex h-9 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-3 font-mono text-xs transition-colors ${
                      showHint
                        ? 'border-amber-500/50 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300'
                        : 'border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-700 sm:bg-[#09090B]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Lightbulb className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>Pista</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Bloque Setup (Inverso) */}
              {showSetup && (
                <div className="mt-2.5 flex w-full max-w-md items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-3 py-2 sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
                      Armar caso desde cubo resuelto
                    </div>
                    <p
                      translate="no"
                      className="mt-0.5 break-words font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-200"
                    >
                      {setupAlg}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCopy(setupAlg, 'setup')}
                    aria-label="Copiar setup inverso"
                    className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
                  >
                    {copiedKey === 'setup' ? (
                      <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#10B981]" />
                    ) : (
                      <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Bloque Pista (sin revelar el algoritmo) */}
              {showHint && !isRevealed && (
                <div className="mt-2.5 w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5 px-3 py-2.5 text-left">
                  {currentCase.auf && (
                    <p className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-300">
                      <span className="text-zinc-500">AUF: </span>
                      {currentCase.auf}
                    </p>
                  )}
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-300">
                    {currentCase.recognition}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Respuesta revelada */}
              {isRevealed ? (
                <div className="mt-3 w-full max-w-md space-y-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] p-3 text-left sm:bg-[#09090B]">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <span
                        translate="no"
                        className="font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#10B981]"
                      >
                        {currentCase.code}
                      </span>
                      <h3 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[#FAFAFA]">
                        {currentCase.name}
                      </h3>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleCopy(displayAlg, 'alg')}
                        aria-label="Copiar algoritmo"
                        className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
                      >
                        {copiedKey === 'alg' ? (
                          <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#10B981]" />
                        ) : (
                          <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          triggerHaptic('selection');
                          onOpenIn3D(currentCase);
                        }}
                        className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-[#FAFAFA] px-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#09090B] transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-200"
                      >
                        <Play className="h-3 w-3 fill-current" />
                        <span>Abrir en 3D</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] p-2.5 sm:bg-[#111113]">
                    <AlgNotationBadges alg={displayAlg} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                    {currentCase.grip && (
                      <span>
                        <span className="text-zinc-600">AGARRE: </span>
                        <span className="text-zinc-200">{currentCase.grip}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {currentCase.auf && (
                      <span>
                        <span className="text-zinc-600">AUF: </span>
                        <span className="text-zinc-200">{currentCase.auf}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-4 max-w-xs text-center text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  Reconoce el patrón, ejecútalo en tu cubo físico y revela para verificar.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Zona de pulgar fija en la parte inferior */}
            <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] px-4 py-3 sm:bg-[#111113]">
              {!isRevealed ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('light');
                    setIsRevealed(true);
                  }}
                  className="inline-flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#FAFAFA] px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-[#09090B] transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-200 active:bg-zinc-300"
                >
                  <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Mostrar algoritmo</span>
                </button>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleGradeReview}
                    className="inline-flex h-12 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/50 bg-amber-500/15 px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300 transition-colors active:bg-amber-500/25"
                  >
                    <Flame className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>Repasar</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleGradeKnown}
                    className="inline-flex h-12 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-[#10B981]/50 bg-[#10B981]/15 px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#10B981] transition-colors active:bg-[#10B981]/25"
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>¡Lo domino!</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

========================================
FILE: src/components/TimerModule.tsx
========================================
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { RefreshCw, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../utils/haptics';

type TimerPhase = 'idle' | 'countdown' | 'ready' | 'running' | 'stopped';

const COUNTDOWN_STEP_MS = 350;

const SCRAMBLE_FACES = ['R', 'L', 'U', 'D', 'F', 'B'] as const;
const SCRAMBLE_AXIS: Record<string, number> = {
  R: 0,
  L: 0,
  U: 1,
  D: 1,
  F: 2,
  B: 2,
};
const SCRAMBLE_MODS = ['', "'", '2'] as const;

function generateWcaScramble(length = 20): string {
  const moves: string[] = [];
  let prevFace = '';
  let prevAxis = -1;
  let prevPrevAxis = -1;

  while (moves.length < length) {
    const face =
      SCRAMBLE_FACES[Math.floor(Math.random() * SCRAMBLE_FACES.length)];
    const axis = SCRAMBLE_AXIS[face];
    if (face === prevFace) continue;
    if (axis === prevAxis && axis === prevPrevAxis) continue;

    const mod = SCRAMBLE_MODS[Math.floor(Math.random() * SCRAMBLE_MODS.length)];
    moves.push(`${face}${mod}`);
    prevPrevAxis = prevAxis;
    prevAxis = axis;
    prevFace = face;
  }

  return moves.join(' ');
}

function formatTimerMs(ms: number): string {
  const safeMs = Math.max(0, ms);
  const totalCentis = Math.floor(safeMs / 10);
  const centis = totalCentis % 100;
  const totalSeconds = Math.floor(totalCentis / 100);
  const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
  const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);

  const cc = String(centis).padStart(2, '0');
  if (minutes > 0) {
    const ss = String(seconds).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${minutes}:${ss}.${cc}`;
  }
  return `${seconds}.${cc}`;
}

/**
 * Cronómetro WCA a pantalla completa (100% en memoria, cero localStorage/sessionStorage/BD).
 * Flujo exacto: Mantener presionado -> 3, 2, 1 (rojo) -> 0 (verde, fijo mientras siga presionado)
 * -> Soltar -> Corre con performance.now() + requestAnimationFrame -> Pulsar/Tocar -> Detiene.
 */
export const TimerModule: React.FC = () => {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<TimerPhase>('idle');
  const [countdownValue, setCountdownValue] = useState<3 | 2 | 1>(3);
  const [elapsedMs, setElapsedMs] = useState<number>(0);
  const [scramble, setScramble] = useState<string>(() => generateWcaScramble());

  const phaseRef = useRef<TimerPhase>('idle');
  const elapsedMsRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const startTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const rafIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const countdownTimersRef = useRef<number[]>([]);
  const justStoppedRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const touchActiveRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const touchResetTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const mouseHoldingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  const syncPhase = useCallback((next: TimerPhase) => {
    phaseRef.current = next;
    setPhase(next);
  }, []);

  const clearCountdownTimers = useCallback(() => {
    for (const id of countdownTimersRef.current) {
      window.clearTimeout(id);
    }
    countdownTimersRef.current = [];
  }, []);

  const stopRaf = useCallback(() => {
    if (rafIdRef.current !== null) {
      window.cancelAnimationFrame(rafIdRef.current);
      rafIdRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const startRunning = useCallback(() => {
    clearCountdownTimers();
    stopRaf();
    startTimeRef.current = performance.now();
    elapsedMsRef.current = 0;
    setElapsedMs(0);
    syncPhase('running');
    triggerHaptic('light');

    const tick = () => {
      if (phaseRef.current !== 'running') return;
      const nowElapsed = performance.now() - startTimeRef.current;
      elapsedMsRef.current = nowElapsed;
      setElapsedMs(nowElapsed);
      rafIdRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    rafIdRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }, [clearCountdownTimers, stopRaf, syncPhase]);

  const stopRunning = useCallback(() => {
    if (phaseRef.current !== 'running') return;
    const finalMs = performance.now() - startTimeRef.current;
    stopRaf();
    elapsedMsRef.current = finalMs;
    setElapsedMs(finalMs);
    justStoppedRef.current = true;
    syncPhase('stopped');
    setScramble(generateWcaScramble());
    triggerHaptic('success');
  }, [stopRaf, syncPhase]);

  const beginHold = useCallback(() => {
    if (phaseRef.current === 'running') {
      stopRunning();
      return;
    }
    if (justStoppedRef.current) return;
    if (phaseRef.current === 'countdown' || phaseRef.current === 'ready') return;

    clearCountdownTimers();
    setCountdownValue(3);
    syncPhase('countdown');
    triggerHaptic('light');

    const t2 = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (phaseRef.current !== 'countdown') return;
      setCountdownValue(2);
      triggerHaptic('light');
    }, COUNTDOWN_STEP_MS);

    const t1 = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (phaseRef.current !== 'countdown') return;
      setCountdownValue(1);
      triggerHaptic('light');
    }, COUNTDOWN_STEP_MS * 2);

    const tReady = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (phaseRef.current !== 'countdown') return;
      clearCountdownTimers();
      syncPhase('ready');
      triggerHaptic('medium');
    }, COUNTDOWN_STEP_MS * 3);

    countdownTimersRef.current = [t2, t1, tReady];
  }, [clearCountdownTimers, stopRunning, syncPhase]);

  const endHold = useCallback(() => {
    if (justStoppedRef.current) {
      justStoppedRef.current = false;
      return;
    }

    if (phaseRef.current === 'ready') {
      startRunning();
      return;
    }

    if (phaseRef.current === 'countdown') {
      clearCountdownTimers();
      syncPhase(elapsedMsRef.current > 0 ? 'stopped' : 'idle');
    }
  }, [clearCountdownTimers, startRunning, syncPhase]);

  const handleReset = useCallback(() => {
    if (phaseRef.current === 'running') return;
    clearCountdownTimers();
    stopRaf();
    justStoppedRef.current = false;
    elapsedMsRef.current = 0;
    setElapsedMs(0);
    syncPhase('idle');
    triggerHaptic('light');
  }, [clearCountdownTimers, stopRaf, syncPhase]);

  const handleRefreshScramble = useCallback(() => {
    if (phaseRef.current === 'running') return;
    setScramble(generateWcaScramble());
    triggerHaptic('selection');
  }, []);

  // Limpieza al desmontar
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      clearCountdownTimers();
      stopRaf();
      if (touchResetTimerRef.current !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimerRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [clearCountdownTimers, stopRaf]);

  // Controles de teclado en PC: Espacio (mantener/soltar/parar), cualquier tecla para detener, R/Esc para reiniciar
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;

      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (
        target &&
        (target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
          target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
          target.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
          target.isContentEditable)
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (phaseRef.current === 'running') {
        e.preventDefault();
        stopRunning();
        return;
      }

      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (e.repeat) return;
        beginHold();
        return;
      }

      if (
        (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R' || e.key === 'Escape') &&
        phaseRef.current === 'stopped' &&
        !e.repeat
      ) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleReset();
      }
    };

    const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (justStoppedRef.current) {
        justStoppedRef.current = false;
        return;
      }
      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        endHold();
      }
    };

    const onBlur = () => {
      mouseHoldingRef.current = false;
      justStoppedRef.current = false;
      if (phaseRef.current === 'countdown' || phaseRef.current === 'ready') {
        clearCountdownTimers();
        syncPhase(elapsedMsRef.current > 0 ? 'stopped' : 'idle');
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    window.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
      window.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
    };
  }, [beginHold, clearCountdownTimers, endHold, handleReset, stopRunning, syncPhase]);

  // Handlers táctiles (móvil)
  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    touchActiveRef.current = true;
    if (touchResetTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimerRef.current);
      touchResetTimerRef.current = null;
    }

    if (phaseRef.current === 'running') {
      stopRunning();
      return;
    }

    if (e.touches.length === 1) {
      beginHold();
    }
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (e.touches.length === 0) {
      endHold();
      touchResetTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        touchActiveRef.current = false;
      }, 450);
    }
  };

  const handleTouchCancel = () => {
    justStoppedRef.current = false;
    clearCountdownTimers();
    if (phaseRef.current === 'countdown' || phaseRef.current === 'ready') {
      syncPhase(elapsedMsRef.current > 0 ? 'stopped' : 'idle');
    }
    touchResetTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      touchActiveRef.current = false;
    }, 450);
  };

  // Soporte opcional de clic mantenido en escritorio (ignorado en dispositivos táctiles)
  const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (touchActiveRef.current || e.button !== 0) return;
    mouseHoldingRef.current = true;
    beginHold();
  };

  const handleMouseUp = () => {
    if (touchActiveRef.current || !mouseHoldingRef.current) return;
    mouseHoldingRef.current = false;
    endHold();
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    if (!mouseHoldingRef.current) return;
    mouseHoldingRef.current = false;
    if (phaseRef.current === 'countdown' || phaseRef.current === 'ready') {
      clearCountdownTimers();
      syncPhase(elapsedMsRef.current > 0 ? 'stopped' : 'idle');
    }
  };

  const isRunning = phase === 'running';
  const isCountdown = phase === 'countdown';
  const isReady = phase === 'ready';
  const isStopped = phase === 'stopped';

  const mainDisplay = isCountdown
    ? String(countdownValue)
    : isReady
    ? '0'
    : formatTimerMs(elapsedMs);

  const mainColorClass = isCountdown
    ? 'text-[#EF4444]'
    : isReady
    ? 'text-[#10B981]'
    : 'text-[#FAFAFA]';

  const statusMeta = isCountdown
    ? {
        dot: 'bg-[#EF4444]',
        badge: 'CONTEO',
        caption: 'Sigue manteniendo presionado hasta llegar a 0…',
      }
    : isReady
    ? {
        dot: 'bg-[#10B981]',
        badge: 'LISTO · 0.00',
        caption: 'Suelta ahora para arrancar el cronómetro',
      }
    : isRunning
    ? {
        dot: 'bg-[#FAFAFA]',
        badge: 'CORRIENDO',
        caption: 'Presiona cualquier tecla o toca la pantalla para detener',
      }
    : isStopped
    ? {
        dot: 'bg-[#3B82F6]',
        badge: 'TIEMPO FINAL',
        caption: 'Mantén Espacio o la pantalla para el siguiente intento',
      }
    : {
        dot: 'bg-zinc-500',
        badge: 'EN ESPERA',
        caption: 'Mantén presionado Espacio o toca la pantalla',
      };

  const stopBubble = (e: React.SyntheticEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
  };

  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Cronómetro de speedcubing"
      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
      onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
      onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
      onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
      onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
      className={`timer-surface h-timer-viewport relative flex w-full flex-1 flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden px-4 py-4 transition-colors sm:px-8 sm:py-8 ${
        isReady
          ? 'bg-[#10B981]/[0.03]'
          : isCountdown
          ? 'bg-[#EF4444]/[0.03]'
          : 'bg-transparent'
      }`}
    >
      {/* Zona superior: Scramble WCA en memoria (se desvanece durante el solve) */}
      <div
        className={`mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col items-center gap-2 transition-opacity duration-150 ${
          isRunning ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400">
          <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${statusMeta.dot}`} />
          <span>{statusMeta.badge}</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-500">SESIÓN EN MEMORIA</span>
        </div>

        <div
          onTouchStart={stopBubble}
          onTouchEnd={stopBubble}
          onMouseDown={stopBubble}
          onMouseUp={stopBubble}
          className="flex max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113]/90 px-3 py-2"
        >
          <p
            translate="no"
            className="text-center font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed tracking-wide text-zinc-300 tabular-nums sm:text-sm"
          >
            {scramble}
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleRefreshScramble}
            aria-label="Nuevo scramble"
            title="Generar nuevo scramble WCA"
            className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#09090B] text-zinc-400 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
          >
            <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Centro: Reloj gigante de precisión con tabular-nums */}
      <div className="my-auto flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 text-center sm:py-6">
        <div
          translate="no"
          aria-live={isRunning ? 'off' : 'polite'}
          className={`font-mono text-[clamp(4.75rem,20vw,10.5rem)] font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight tabular-nums transition-colors duration-100 ${mainColorClass}`}
        >
          {mainDisplay}
        </div>

        {/* Indicador visual discreto de pasos 3 -> 2 -> 1 -> 0 */}
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={`mt-5 flex items-center gap-2 transition-opacity duration-150 ${
            isRunning ? 'opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'
          }`}
        >
          {[3, 2, 1].map((step) => {
            const passed = isReady || (isCountdown && countdownValue <= step);
            return (
              <span
                key={step}
                className={`h-1.5 w-7 rounded-full transition-colors duration-100 ${
                  isReady
                    ? 'bg-[#10B981]'
                    : passed
                    ? 'bg-[#EF4444]'
                    : 'bg-zinc-800'
                }`}
              />
            );
          })}
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 w-7 rounded-full transition-colors duration-100 ${
              isReady ? 'bg-[#10B981]' : 'bg-zinc-800'
            }`}
          />
        </div>

        <p
          className={`mt-4 font-mono text-xs sm:text-sm ${
            isReady
              ? 'font-medium text-[#10B981]'
              : isCountdown
              ? 'font-medium text-[#EF4444]'
              : 'text-zinc-500'
          }`}
        >
          {statusMeta.caption}
        </p>

        {/* Botón rápido de reinicio en memoria cuando está detenido */}
        <div
          onTouchStart={stopBubble}
          onTouchEnd={stopBubble}
          onMouseDown={stopBubble}
          onMouseUp={stopBubble}
          className={`mt-5 min-h-[2.5rem] transition-opacity duration-150 ${
            isStopped ? 'opacity-100' : 'pointer-events-none opacity-0'
          }`}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleReset}
            tabIndex={isStopped ? 0 : -1}
            className="inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-[#222226] bg-[#111113] px-3.5 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-[#16161A] hover:text-[#FAFAFA]"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>Reiniciar a 0.00</span>
            <kbd className="hidden rounded border border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090B] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-zinc-400 lg:inline">
              R
            </kbd>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Zona inferior: Indicaciones discretas de uso (PC vs Móvil) */}
      <div
        className={`mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-center font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500 transition-opacity duration-150 ${
          isRunning ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Indicaciones Desktop */}
        <div className="hidden flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4 lg:flex">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <kbd className="rounded border border-zinc-800 bg-[#111113] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-zinc-300">
              Espacio
            </kbd>
            <span>Mantener (3 → 2 → 1 → 0 verde)</span>
          </span>
          <span className="text-zinc-800">|</span>
          <span>Soltar en 0 verde para iniciar</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-800">|</span>
          <span>Cualquier tecla para detener</span>
        </div>

        {/* Indicaciones Móvil */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 lg:hidden">
          <span>Mantén tocada la pantalla (3 → 2 → 1 → 0)</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
          <span>Suelta en verde</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
          <span>Toca para parar</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

========================================
FILE: src/data/cfopData.ts

import type { CfopCase, LearningStage, NotationCategory, NotationMove } from '../types/cfop';
import {
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CASES,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CHAPTERS,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CORE_IDS,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_SUBCATEGORIES,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_VIDEO_URL,
} from './cfopReducidoData';
import { ESSENTIAL_F2L_IDS, F2L_CASES, F2L_SUBCATEGORIES } from './f2lData';
import { OLL_CASES, OLL_SUBCATEGORIES, TWO_LOOK_OLL_IDS } from './ollData';
import { PLL_CASES, PLL_SUBCATEGORIES, TWO_LOOK_PLL_IDS } from './pllData';

export {
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CASES,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CHAPTERS,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_CORE_IDS,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_SUBCATEGORIES,
  CFOP_REDUCIDO_VIDEO_URL,
  ESSENTIAL_F2L_IDS,
  F2L_CASES,
  F2L_SUBCATEGORIES,
  OLL_CASES,
  OLL_SUBCATEGORIES,
  PLL_CASES,
  PLL_SUBCATEGORIES,
  TWO_LOOK_OLL_IDS,
  TWO_LOOK_PLL_IDS,
};

export interface NotationRuleCard {
  symbol: string;
  title: string;
  badge: string;
  explanation: string;
  example: string;
}

export const NOTATION_RULES: NotationRuleCard[] = [

      'Gira esa cara 90 grados en el sentido de las agujas del reloj, como si estuvieras mirando esa cara directamente de frente.',
    example: 'R = Cara derecha 90° horario (hacia arriba desde el frente).',
  },
  {

export const NOTATION_CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<
  NotationCategory,

========================================
FILE: src/data/cfopReducidoData.ts

import type { CfopCase } from '../types/cfop';

export const CFOP_REDUCIDO_VIDEO_URL =
  'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-5i7z_PJWy4';

export function buildYoutubeTimestampUrl(mmss: string): string {
  const [mStr, sStr] = mmss.split(':');
  const seconds = (Number(mStr) || 0) * 60 + (Number(sStr) || 0);
  return `${CFOP_REDUCIDO_VIDEO_URL}&t=${seconds}s`;
}

/**
 * Normaliza notación de guías informales (´, X/Y mayúsculas, espacios múltiples)
 * a notación WCA limpia 100% compatible con <twisty-player>.
 */
export function normalizeWcaAlg(raw: string): string {
  return raw
    .replace(/[´’`]/g, "'")
    .replace(/\bX('?|2?)\b/g, 'x$1')
    .replace(/\bY('?|2?)\b/g, 'y$1')
    .replace(/\bZ('?|2?)\b/g, 'z$1')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

import type { CfopCase } from '../types/cfop';

export const F2L_SUBCATEGORIES: string[] = [
  'Todos',
  'Casos básicos',

  'Juntas en capa U',

FILE: src/hooks/useCaseMastery.ts
========================================
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { CaseMastery } from '../types/cfop';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../utils/haptics';

const MASTERY_STORAGE_KEY = 'cfop_case_mastery_v1';
const LEGACY_REDUCIDO_KEY = 'cfop_reducido_learned';

function isValidMastery(value: unknown): value is CaseMastery {
  return value === 'unlearned' || value === 'practicing' || value === 'mastered';
}

function loadInitialMastery(): Record<string, CaseMastery> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.localStorage) return {};

  const map: Record<string, CaseMastery> = {};
  let migrated = false;

  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(MASTERY_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
          if (typeof k === 'string' && isValidMastery(v) && v !== 'unlearned') {
            map[k] = v;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore corrupted storage
  }

  // Migración no destructiva desde la clave previa de CFOP Reducido
  try {
    const legacyRaw = window.localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_REDUCIDO_KEY);
    if (legacyRaw) {
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(legacyRaw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        for (const item of parsed) {
          if (typeof item === 'string' && !(item in map)) {
            map[item] = 'mastered';
            migrated = true;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore corrupted legacy storage
  }

  if (migrated) {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(MASTERY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage quota errors
    }
  }

  return map;
}

function persistMastery(map: Record<string, CaseMastery>): void {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
      window.localStorage.setItem(MASTERY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage quota errors
  }
}

export function getNextMasteryState(current: CaseMastery): CaseMastery {
  if (current === 'unlearned') return 'practicing';
  if (current === 'practicing') return 'mastered';
  return 'unlearned';
}

export function useCaseMastery() {
  const [masteryMap, setMasteryMap] =
    useState<Record<string, CaseMastery>>(loadInitialMastery);

  const getMastery = useCallback(
    (id: string): CaseMastery => masteryMap[id] ?? 'unlearned',
    [masteryMap]
  );

  const setMastery = useCallback((id: string, status: CaseMastery) => {
    setMasteryMap((prev) => {
      const current = prev[id] ?? 'unlearned';
      if (current === status) return prev;
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (status === 'unlearned') {
        delete next[id];
      } else {
        next[id] = status;
      }
      persistMastery(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const cycleMastery = useCallback(
    (id: string): CaseMastery => {
      const current = masteryMap[id] ?? 'unlearned';
      const nextStatus = getNextMasteryState(current);
      if (nextStatus === 'mastered') {
        triggerHaptic('success');
      } else if (nextStatus === 'practicing') {
        triggerHaptic('selection');
      } else {
        triggerHaptic('light');
      }
      setMastery(id, nextStatus);
      return nextStatus;
    },
    [masteryMap, setMastery]
  );

  return {
    masteryMap,
    getMastery,
    setMastery,
    cycleMastery,
  };
}

FILE: src/utils/algHelpers.ts

export interface TriggerSegment {
  type: 'trigger' | 'moves';
  moves: string[];
  startIndex: number;
  triggerName?: string;
}

export interface MoveVisualStyle {
  isPrime: boolean;
  isDouble: boolean;
  isWideOrRotation: boolean;
  isRotation: boolean;
}

interface TriggerPattern {
  name: string;
  sequence: string[];
}

const TRIGGER_PATTERNS: TriggerPattern[] = [
  {
    name: 'Sune',
    sequence: ['R', 'U', "R'", 'U', 'R', 'U2', "R'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Antisune',
    sequence: ['R', 'U2', "R'", "U'", 'R', "U'", "R'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Sexy Move',
    sequence: ['R', 'U', "R'", "U'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Wide Sexy',
    sequence: ['r', 'U', "R'", "U'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Inverse Sexy',
    sequence: ['U', 'R', "U'", "R'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Left Sexy',
    sequence: ["L'", "U'", 'L', 'U'],
  },
  {
    name: 'Sledgehammer',
    sequence: ["R'", 'F', 'R', "F'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Hedgeslammer',
    sequence: ['F', "R'", "F'", 'R'],
  },
  {
    name: 'Insert (R U\' R\')',
    sequence: ['R', "U'", "R'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Insert (R U R\')',
    sequence: ['R', 'U', "R'"],
  },
  {
    name: 'Insert F',
    sequence: ["F'", "U'", 'F'],
  },
];

const SORTED_PATTERNS = [...TRIGGER_PATTERNS].sort(
  (a, b) => b.sequence.length - a.sequence.length
);

export function splitAlgMoves(alg: string): string[] {
  return alg
    .trim()
    .replace(/[()[\]]/g, ' ')
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean);
}

export function countAlgMoves(alg: string): number {
  return splitAlgMoves(alg).length;
}

export function classifyMoveToken(move: string): MoveVisualStyle {
  const m = move.match(/^([RLUDFBrludfbMESxyz])(2|')?$/);
  const base = m ? m[1] : move[0] ?? '';
  const mod = m ? m[2] ?? '' : '';
  const isRotation = base === 'x' || base === 'y' || base === 'z';
  const isWide =
    base === 'r' ||
    base === 'l' ||
    base === 'u' ||
    base === 'd' ||
    base === 'f' ||
    base === 'b';

  return {
    isPrime: mod === "'",
    isDouble: mod === '2',
    isWideOrRotation: isWide || isRotation,
    isRotation,
  };
}

export function segmentAlgWithTriggers(alg: string): TriggerSegment[] {
  const tokens = splitAlgMoves(alg);
  const segments: TriggerSegment[] = [];
  let i = 0;

  while (i < tokens.length) {
    let matched: TriggerPattern | null = null;

    for (const pattern of SORTED_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.sequence.length === 3 && tokens.length <= 3) continue;
      const len = pattern.sequence.length;
      if (i + len <= tokens.length) {
        let ok = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < len; j++) {
          if (tokens[i + j] !== pattern.sequence[j]) {
            ok = false;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (ok) {
          matched = pattern;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (matched) {
      segments.push({
        type: 'trigger',
        moves: tokens.slice(i, i + matched.sequence.length),
        startIndex: i,
        triggerName: matched.name,
      });
      i += matched.sequence.length;
    } else {
      const last = segments[segments.length - 1];
      if (last && last.type === 'moves') {
        last.moves.push(tokens[i]);
      } else {
        segments.push({
          type: 'moves',
          moves: [tokens[i]],
          startIndex: i,
        });
      }
      i += 1;
    }
  }

  return segments;
}

/**
 * Refleja un algoritmo Izquierda <-> Derecha (R <-> L, r <-> l, invierte sentido de U/D/F/B/y/z/S/E).
 * M y x mantienen su sentido simple porque giran en el mismo plano sagital (YZ) perpendicular al espejo L<->R.
 */
export function mirrorAlgLR(alg: string): string {
  const tokens = splitAlgMoves(alg);

  const swapBase: Record<string, string> = {
    R: 'L',
    L: 'R',
    r: 'l',
    l: 'r',
    U: 'U',
    u: 'u',
    D: 'D',
    d: 'd',
    F: 'F',
    f: 'f',
    B: 'B',
    b: 'b',
    M: 'M',
    E: 'E',
    S: 'S',
    x: 'x',
    y: 'y',
    z: 'z',
  };

  const sameDirectionOnMirror = new Set(['M', 'x']);

  return tokens
    .map((token) => {
      const match = token.match(/^([RLUDFBrludfbMESxyz])(2|')?$/);
      if (!match) return token;
      const [, base, mod = ''] = match;
      const mappedBase = swapBase[base] ?? base;
      if (mod === '2') {
        return `${mappedBase}2`;
      }
      if (sameDirectionOnMirror.has(base)) {
        return `${mappedBase}${mod}`;
      }
      const invertedMod = mod === "'" ? '' : "'";
      return `${mappedBase}${invertedMod}`;
    })
    .join(' ');
}

/**
 * Calcula el inverso exacto WCA de un algoritmo (para hacer Setup desde un cubo resuelto).
 * Invierte el orden de los giros y cambia el sentido de giros simples ('' <-> "'"), conservando '2'.
 */
export function invertAlg(alg: string): string {
  const tokens = splitAlgMoves(alg);
  return [...tokens]
    .reverse()
    .map((token) => {
      const match = token.match(/^([RLUDFBrludfbMESxyz])(2|')?$/);
      if (!match) return token;
      const [, base, mod = ''] = match;
      if (mod === '2') return `${base}2`;
      return mod === "'" ? base : `${base}'`;
    })
    .join(' ');
}

import type { TopFaceColor, VisualStage } from '../types/cfop';
import { splitAlgMoves } from './algHelpers';

export type FaceCode = 'U' | 'D' | 'F' | 'B' | 'R' | 'L';

export interface StickerCell {
  face: FaceCode;
  /** Hex color ready to render in SVG */
  color: string;
  /** True if dimmed/masked out for the current CFOP stage */
  masked: boolean;
}

export interface PllArrow {
  fromRow: number;
  fromCol: number;
  toRow: number;
  toCol: number;
  bidirectional: boolean;
  isCorner: boolean;
}

interface Vec3 {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
}

interface StickerState {
  normal: Vec3;
  homeFace: FaceCode;
  isPrimaryU: boolean;
}

interface CubieState {
  pos: Vec3;
  homePos: Vec3;
  stickers: StickerState[];
}

const FACE_COLORS_BY_TOP: Record<TopFaceColor, Record<FaceCode, string>> = {
  // Orientación estándar CFOP (z2 desde WCA): Amarilla arriba (U), Blanca abajo (D), Verde al frente (F)
  yellow: {
    U: '#EAB308', // Amarillo Speedcube
    D: '#FAFAFA', // Blanco WCA
    F: '#10B981', // Verde Speedcube
    B: '#3B82F6', // Azul Speedcube
    R: '#F97316', // Naranja Speedcube
    L: '#EF4444', // Rojo Speedcube
  },
  // Orientación alternativa: Blanca arriba (U), Amarilla abajo (D), Verde al frente (F)
  white: {
    U: '#FAFAFA', // Blanco WCA
    D: '#EAB308', // Amarillo Speedcube
    F: '#10B981', // Verde Speedcube
    B: '#3B82F6', // Azul Speedcube
    R: '#EF4444', // Rojo Speedcube
    L: '#F97316', // Naranja Speedcube
  },
};

const DIM_FACE_COLORS_BY_TOP: Record<TopFaceColor, Record<FaceCode, string>> = {
  yellow: {
    U: '#854D0E',
    D: '#71717A',
    F: '#065F46',
    B: '#1E3A8A',
    R: '#7C2D12',
    L: '#7F1D1D',
  },
  white: {
    U: '#71717A',
    D: '#854D0E',
    F: '#065F46',
    B: '#1E3A8A',
    R: '#7F1D1D',
    L: '#7C2D12',
  },
};

const IGNORED_COLOR = '#1F1F24';

function createSolvedCube(): CubieState[] {
  const cubies: CubieState[] = [];
  for (let x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
    for (let y = -1; y <= 1; y++) {
      for (let z = -1; z <= 1; z++) {
        if (x === 0 && y === 0 && z === 0) continue;
        const stickers: StickerState[] = [];
        if (y === 1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: 0, y: 1, z: 0 },
            homeFace: 'U',
            isPrimaryU: true,
          });
        }
        if (y === -1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: 0, y: -1, z: 0 },
            homeFace: 'D',
            isPrimaryU: false,
          });
        }
        if (z === 1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 },
            homeFace: 'F',
            isPrimaryU: false,
          });
        }
        if (z === -1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: -1 },
            homeFace: 'B',
            isPrimaryU: false,
          });
        }
        if (x === 1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 },
            homeFace: 'R',
            isPrimaryU: false,
          });
        }
        if (x === -1) {
          stickers.push({
            normal: { x: -1, y: 0, z: 0 },
            homeFace: 'L',
            isPrimaryU: false,
          });
        }
        cubies.push({
          pos: { x, y, z },
          homePos: { x, y, z },
          stickers,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return cubies;
}

function rotateVec(v: Vec3, axis: 'x' | 'y' | 'z', dir: 1 | -1): Vec3 {
  if (axis === 'x') {
    return dir === 1
      ? { x: v.x, y: v.z, z: -v.y }
      : { x: v.x, y: -v.z, z: v.y };
  }
  if (axis === 'y') {
    return dir === 1
      ? { x: -v.z, y: v.y, z: v.x }
      : { x: v.z, y: v.y, z: -v.x };
  }
  return dir === 1
    ? { x: v.y, y: -v.x, z: v.z }
    : { x: -v.y, y: v.x, z: v.z };
}

function applySingleQuantumMove(
  cubies: CubieState[],
  base: string,
  prime: boolean
): void {
  let axis: 'x' | 'y' | 'z' = 'x';
  let baseDir: 1 | -1 = 1;
  let matchLayer: (p: Vec3) => boolean = () => false;

  switch (base) {
    case 'R':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.x === 1;
      break;
    case 'r':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.x >= 0;
      break;
    case 'L':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.x === -1;
      break;
    case 'l':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.x <= 0;
      break;
    case 'M':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.x === 0;
      break;
    case 'x':
      axis = 'x';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = () => true;
      break;

    case 'U':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.y === 1;
      break;
    case 'u':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.y >= 0;
      break;
    case 'D':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.y === -1;
      break;
    case 'd':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.y <= 0;
      break;
    case 'E':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.y === 0;
      break;
    case 'y':
      axis = 'y';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = () => true;
      break;

    case 'F':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.z === 1;
      break;
    case 'f':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.z >= 0;
      break;
    case 'B':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.z === -1;
      break;
    case 'b':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = -1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.z <= 0;
      break;
    case 'S':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = (p) => p.z === 0;
      break;
    case 'z':
      axis = 'z';
      baseDir = 1;
      matchLayer = () => true;
      break;
    default:
      return;
  }

  const dir: 1 | -1 = prime ? (baseDir === 1 ? -1 : 1) : baseDir;

  for (const cubie of cubies) {
    if (matchLayer(cubie.pos)) {
      cubie.pos = rotateVec(cubie.pos, axis, dir);
      for (const st of cubie.stickers) {
        st.normal = rotateVec(st.normal, axis, dir);
      }
    }
  }
}

function invertToken(token: string): string {
  const m = token.match(/^([RLUDFBrludfbMESxyz])(2|')?$/);
  if (!m) return token;
  const [, base, mod = ''] = m;
  if (mod === '2') return `${base}2`;
  return mod === "'" ? base : `${base}'`;
}

/**
 * Reorienta únicamente el eje vertical para mantener la cara U arriba (0,1,0)
 * cuando un algoritmo tiene giros wide/slice/x/z sin cerrar, preservando
 * la rotación horizontal (y / y' / y2) para coincidir con <twisty-player>
 * y mostrar siempre el slot frontal-derecho (FR) en F2L.
 */
function alignUOnTop(cubies: CubieState[]): void {
  const uPos = cubies.find(
    (c) => c.homePos.x === 0 && c.homePos.y === 1 && c.homePos.z === 0
  )?.pos;
  if (!uPos) return;

  if (uPos.y === -1) {
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'x', false);
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'x', false);
  } else if (uPos.z === 1) {
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'x', false);
  } else if (uPos.z === -1) {
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'x', true);
  } else if (uPos.x === 1) {
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'z', true);
  } else if (uPos.x === -1) {
    applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, 'z', false);
  }
}

interface HorizontalFrame {
  /** Current position of home F-center (0,0,1) */
  cF: Vec3;
  /** Current position of home R-center (1,0,0) */
  cR: Vec3;
  /** Home coordinates (hx, hz) of the slot currently sitting at Front-Right (1, *, 1) */
  frHomeX: number;
  frHomeZ: number;
}

function getHorizontalFrame(cubies: CubieState[]): HorizontalFrame {
  const cF =
    cubies.find(
      (c) => c.homePos.x === 0 && c.homePos.y === 0 && c.homePos.z === 1
    )?.pos ?? { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 };
  const cR =
    cubies.find(
      (c) => c.homePos.x === 1 && c.homePos.y === 0 && c.homePos.z === 0
    )?.pos ?? { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 };

  const frontCenter = cubies.find(
    (c) => c.pos.x === 0 && c.pos.y === 0 && c.pos.z === 1
  );
  const rightCenter = cubies.find(
    (c) => c.pos.x === 1 && c.pos.y === 0 && c.pos.z === 0
  );

  const frHomeX =
    (frontCenter?.homePos.x ?? 0) + (rightCenter?.homePos.x ?? 1);
  const frHomeZ =
    (frontCenter?.homePos.z ?? 1) + (rightCenter?.homePos.z ?? 0);

  return { cF, cR, frHomeX, frHomeZ };
}

function getStickerColorForStage(
  cubie: CubieState,
  sticker: StickerState,
  stage: VisualStage,
  frame: HorizontalFrame,
  topFace: TopFaceColor
): StickerCell {
  const palette = FACE_COLORS_BY_TOP[topFace];
  const dimPalette = DIM_FACE_COLORS_BY_TOP[topFace];
  const topPrimaryColor = palette.U;

  const { homePos } = cubie;
  const isUPiece = homePos.y === 1;
  const isCrossPiece =
    homePos.y === -1 && (homePos.x === 0 || homePos.z === 0);
  const isF2LPiece = homePos.y <= 0;

  if (stage === 'OLL') {
    if (isUPiece) {
      if (sticker.isPrimaryU) {
        return {
          face: sticker.homeFace,
          color: topPrimaryColor,
          masked: false,
        };
      }
      return { face: sticker.homeFace, color: IGNORED_COLOR, masked: true };
    }
    return {
      face: sticker.homeFace,
      color: dimPalette[sticker.homeFace],
      masked: true,
    };
  }

  if (stage === 'PLL') {
    if (isUPiece) {
      if (sticker.isPrimaryU) {
        return {
          face: sticker.homeFace,
          color: topPrimaryColor,
          masked: false,
        };
      }
      return {
        face: sticker.homeFace,
        color: palette[sticker.homeFace],
        masked: false,
      };
    }
    return {
      face: sticker.homeFace,
      color: dimPalette[sticker.homeFace],
      masked: true,
    };
  }

  if (stage === 'F2L') {
    if (isF2LPiece) {
      const isTargetFRPair =
        homePos.x === frame.frHomeX &&
        homePos.z === frame.frHomeZ &&
        homePos.y <= 0;
      return {
        face: sticker.homeFace,
        color: palette[sticker.homeFace],
        masked: !isTargetFRPair,
      };
    }
    return { face: sticker.homeFace, color: IGNORED_COLOR, masked: true };
  }

  // stage === 'Cross'
  if (isCrossPiece) {
    return {
      face: sticker.homeFace,
      color: palette[sticker.homeFace],
      masked: false,
    };
  }
  const isCenter =
    Math.abs(homePos.x) + Math.abs(homePos.y) + Math.abs(homePos.z) === 1;
  if (isCenter) {
    return {
      face: sticker.homeFace,
      color: dimPalette[sticker.homeFace],
      masked: true,
    };
  }
  return { face: sticker.homeFace, color: IGNORED_COLOR, masked: true };
}

function computePllArrows(
  cubies: CubieState[],
  frame: HorizontalFrame
): PllArrow[] {
  const { cF, cR } = frame;
  const rawMoves: Array<{
    fromRow: number;
    fromCol: number;
    toRow: number;
    toCol: number;
    isCorner: boolean;
  }> = [];

  for (const cubie of cubies) {
    if (cubie.pos.y !== 1 || cubie.homePos.y !== 1) continue;
    const fromRow = cubie.pos.z + 1;
    const fromCol = cubie.pos.x + 1;

    // Target position relative to current horizontal center orientation (supports y / y' / y2)
    const targetX = cubie.homePos.x * cR.x + cubie.homePos.z * cF.x;
    const targetZ = cubie.homePos.x * cR.z + cubie.homePos.z * cF.z;
    const toRow = targetZ + 1;
    const toCol = targetX + 1;

    if (fromRow === toRow && fromCol === toCol) continue;

    const isCorner = Math.abs(cubie.pos.x) === 1 && Math.abs(cubie.pos.z) === 1;
    rawMoves.push({ fromRow, fromCol, toRow, toCol, isCorner });
  }

  const used = new Set<number>();
  const arrows: PllArrow[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < rawMoves.length; i++) {
    if (used.has(i)) continue;
    const m = rawMoves[i];
    const reverseIdx = rawMoves.findIndex(
      (other, idx) =>
        idx > i &&
        !used.has(idx) &&
        other.fromRow === m.toRow &&
        other.fromCol === m.toCol &&
        other.toRow === m.fromRow &&
        other.toCol === m.fromCol
    );

    if (reverseIdx !== -1) {
      used.add(i);
      used.add(reverseIdx);
      arrows.push({
        fromRow: m.fromRow,
        fromCol: m.fromCol,
        toRow: m.toRow,
        toCol: m.toCol,
        bidirectional: true,
        isCorner: m.isCorner,
      });
    } else {
      used.add(i);
      arrows.push({
        fromRow: m.fromRow,
        fromCol: m.fromCol,
        toRow: m.toRow,
        toCol: m.toCol,
        bidirectional: false,
        isCorner: m.isCorner,
      });
    }
  }

  return arrows;
}

export interface SimulatedCaseView {
  /** 3x3 top face U: rows z = -1..1 (back to front), cols x = -1..1 (left to right) */
  topGrid: StickerCell[][];
  /** Top row of B (x = -1..1) viewed from top */
  backTopRow: StickerCell[];
  /** Top row of F (x = -1..1) viewed from top */
  frontTopRow: StickerCell[];
  /** Top row of L (z = -1..1) viewed from top */
  leftTopRow: StickerCell[];
  /** Top row of R (z = -1..1) viewed from top */
  rightTopRow: StickerCell[];
  /** Full 3x3 front face F: rows y = 1..-1 (top to bottom), cols x = -1..1 (left to right) */
  frontGrid: StickerCell[][];
  /** Full 3x3 right face R: rows y = 1..-1 (top to bottom), cols z = 1..-1 (front to back) */
  rightGrid: StickerCell[][];
  /** Exact permutation vectors for PLL 2D diagram */
  pllArrows: PllArrow[];
}

/**
 * Simula en 0ms el estado inicial previo a resolver `alg` (equivalente a experimentalSetupAnchor="end")
 * en TypeScript puro, sin WebGL ni custom elements.
 */
export function simulateCasePreState(
  alg: string,
  stage: VisualStage,
  topFace: TopFaceColor = 'yellow'
): SimulatedCaseView {
  const cubies = createSolvedCube();
  const tokens = splitAlgMoves(alg);
  const inverseTokens = [...tokens].reverse().map(invertToken);

  for (const tok of inverseTokens) {
    const m = tok.match(/^([RLUDFBrludfbMESxyz])(2|')?$/);
    if (!m) continue;
    const [, base, mod = ''] = m;
    if (mod === '2') {
      applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, base, false);
      applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, base, false);
    } else {
      applySingleQuantumMove(cubies, base, mod === "'");
    }
  }

  alignUOnTop(cubies);
  const frame = getHorizontalFrame(cubies);

  const fallbackCell: StickerCell = {
    face: 'U',
    color: IGNORED_COLOR,
    masked: true,
  };

  const findSticker = (
    x: number,
    y: number,
    z: number,
    nx: number,
    ny: number,
    nz: number
  ): StickerCell => {
    const cubie = cubies.find(
      (c) => c.pos.x === x && c.pos.y === y && c.pos.z === z
    );
    if (!cubie) return fallbackCell;
    const st = cubie.stickers.find(
      (s) => s.normal.x === nx && s.normal.y === ny && s.normal.z === nz
    );
    if (!st) return fallbackCell;
    return getStickerColorForStage(cubie, st, stage, frame, topFace);
  };

  const topGrid: StickerCell[][] = [-1, 0, 1].map((z) =>
    [-1, 0, 1].map((x) => findSticker(x, 1, z, 0, 1, 0))
  );

  const backTopRow: StickerCell[] = [-1, 0, 1].map((x) =>
    findSticker(x, 1, -1, 0, 0, -1)
  );
  const frontTopRow: StickerCell[] = [-1, 0, 1].map((x) =>
    findSticker(x, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1)
  );
  const leftTopRow: StickerCell[] = [-1, 0, 1].map((z) =>
    findSticker(-1, 1, z, -1, 0, 0)
  );
  const rightTopRow: StickerCell[] = [-1, 0, 1].map((z) =>
    findSticker(1, 1, z, 1, 0, 0)
  );

  const frontGrid: StickerCell[][] = [1, 0, -1].map((y) =>
    [-1, 0, 1].map((x) => findSticker(x, y, 1, 0, 0, 1))
  );

  const rightGrid: StickerCell[][] = [1, 0, -1].map((y) =>
    [1, 0, -1].map((z) => findSticker(1, y, z, 1, 0, 0))
  );

  const pllArrows = stage === 'PLL' ? computePllArrows(cubies, frame) : [];

  return {
    topGrid,
    backTopRow,
    frontTopRow,
    leftTopRow,
    rightTopRow,
    frontGrid,
    rightGrid,
    pllArrows,
  };
}

/**
 * Utilidad de feedback táctil (Web Haptics) para dispositivos compatibles.
 * Totalmente seguro: no produce errores en navegadores de escritorio ni en WebKit sin API.
 */
export type HapticType = 'light' | 'medium' | 'selection' | 'success';

export function triggerHaptic(type: HapticType = 'light'): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof navigator === 'undefined') return;

  try {
    if ('vibrate' in navigator && typeof navigator.vibrate === 'function') {
      switch (type) {
        case 'selection':
        case 'light':
          navigator.vibrate(6);
          break;
        case 'medium':
          navigator.vibrate(14);
          break;
        case 'success':
          navigator.vibrate([8, 30, 12]);
          break;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Silently ignore if permission is restricted or unsupported
  }
}
